  ===== dbo.Mx_VEW_RBDailyAtd (VIEW) =====
CREATE VIEW [Mx_VEW_RBDailyAtd] AS  SELECT  Mx_DATDTrn.RHalf1 AS [1st Half Status], Mx_DATDTrn.RHalf2 AS [2nd Half Status], Mx_UserMst.AadharNo AS [Aadhar No.], Mx_DATDTrn.ATDAuthBy AS [Attendance Auth By], Mx_DATDTrn.ATDAuthDate AS [Attendance Auth Date], Mx_DATDTrn.ATDAUTHFlg AS [Attendance Auth Flag], Mx_DATDTrn.PDate AS [Attendance Date], Mx_DATDTrn.AUTHCOFFDate AS [Auth C-OFF Date], CASE  	WHEN LEN(CAST(CAST(Mx_DATDTrn.AUTHCOFF / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6))) > 1 		THEN CAST(CAST(Mx_DATDTrn.AUTHCOFF / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6)) 	ELSE 		RIGHT(REPLICATE('0',2) + CAST(CAST(Mx_DATDTrn.AUTHCOFF 		RIGHT(REPLICATE('0', 2) + CAST(CAST(Mx_VEW_OvertimeDet.OVERTIME5 / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6)) ,2) END + ':' + RIGHT(REPLICATE('0', 2) + CAST(Mx_VEW_OvertimeDet.OVERTIME5 - CAST(Mx_VEW_OvertimeDet.OVERTIME5 / 60 AS INT) * 60 AS VARCHAR(6)), 2) AS [OT5-HHMM], Mx_VEW_OvertimeDet.OVERTIME5 AS [OT5-Mins], CASE  	WHEN LEN(CAST(CAST(Mx_DATDTrn.OutGrace / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6))) > 1 		THEN CAST(CAST(Mx_DATDTrn.OutGrace / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6))  	ELSE 		RIGHT(REPLICATE('0', 2) + CAST(CAST(Mx_DATDTrn.OutGrace / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6)) ,2) END + ':' + VARCHAR(6)), 2) AS [Out Time-HHMM], Mx_DATDTrn.OutTime AS [Out Time-Mins], ROUT1.Reason AS [OUT-Reason 1], Mx_DATDTrn.ROUTId1 AS [OUT-Reason 1 ID], ROUT2.Reason AS [OUT-Reason 2], Mx_DATDTrn.ROUTId2 CAST(Mx_DATDTrn.Overstay - CAST(Mx_DATDTrn.Overstay / 60 AS INT) * 60 AS VARCHAR(6)), 2) AS [Overstay-HHMM], Mx_DATDTrn.Overstay AS [Overstay-Mins], Mx_UserMst.PAN AS [PAN No.], Mx_UserMst.PassportNo AS [Passport No.], Mx_UserMst.PERMADD1 AS [Permanent Address 1], Mx_UserMst.PERMADD2 AS [Permanent Address 2], Mx_UserMst.PERMADD3 AS [Permanent Address 3], Mx_UserMst.PERMCity AS [Permanent City], VARCHAR(6))  	ELSE 		RIGHT(REPLICATE('0', 2) + CAST(CAST(Mx_DATDTrn.WrkHrsWithinSft / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6)) ,2) END + ':' + RIGHT(REPLICATE('0', 2) + CAST(Mx_DATDTrn.WrkHrsWithinSft - Working.SFTSTTime AS [Wrk Shift Start], Mx_DATDTrn.ShiftType AS [Wrk Shift Type] FROM Mx_DATDTrn INNER JOIN Mx_UserMst ON Mx_DATDTrn.UserID = Mx_UserMst.UserID INNER JOIN Mx_BranchMst ON Mx_DATDTrn.BRCID = Mx_BranchMst.BRCID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_VEW_OvertimeDet ON Mx_DATDTrn.UserID = Mx_VEW_OvertimeDet.userid AND Mx_DATDTrn.PDate = Mx_VEW_OvertimeDet.pdate INNER JOIN Mx_CategoryMst ON Mx_DATDTrn.CTGID = Mx_CategoryMst.CTGID INNER JOIN Mx_CustomGroup1Mst ON Mx_DATDTrn.CG1ID = Mx_CustomGroup1Mst.CG1ID INNER JOIN Mx_CustomGroup2Mst ON Mx_DATDTrn.CG2ID = Mx_CustomGroup2Mst.CG2ID INNER JOIN Mx_CustomGroup3Mst ON Mx_DATDTrn.CG3ID = Mx_CustomGroup3Mst.CG3ID INNER JOIN Mx_DepartmentMst ON Mx_DATDTrn.DPTID = Mx_DepartmentMst.DPTID INNER JOIN Mx_DesignationMst ON Mx_DATDTrn.DSGID = Mx_DesignationMst.DSGID INNER JOIN Mx_GradeMst ON Mx_DATDTrn.GRDID = Mx_GradeMst.GRDID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_ErrorMst ON Mx_DATDTrn.ErrorID = Mx_ErrorMst.ErrID INNER JOIN Mx_OrganizationMst ON Mx_DATDTrn.ORGID = Mx_OrganizationMst.ORGID INNER JOIN Mx_SectionMst ON Mx_DATDTrn.SECID = Mx_SectionMst.SECID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_RepoGroupMst ON Mx_UserMst.RGID = Mx_RepoGroupMst.RGID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS RIN1 ON Mx_DATDTrn.RINId1 = RIN1.RID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS RIN2 ON Mx_DATDTrn.RINId2 = RIN2.RID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS RIN3 ON Mx_DATDTrn.RINId3 = RIN3.RID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS RIN4 ON Mx_DATDTrn.RINId4 = RIN4.RID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS RIN5 ON Mx_DATDTrn.RINId5 = RIN5.RID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS RIN6 ON Mx_DATDTrn.RINId6 = RIN6.RID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS ROUT1 ON Mx_DATDTrn.ROUTId1 = ROUT1.RID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS ROUT2 ON Mx_DATDTrn.ROUTId2 = ROUT2.RID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS ROUT3 ON Mx_DATDTrn.ROUTId3 = ROUT3.RID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS ROUT4 ON Mx_DATDTrn.ROUTId4 = ROUT4.RID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS ROUT5 ON Mx_DATDTrn.ROUTId5 = ROUT5.RID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS ROUT6 ON Mx_DATDTrn.ROUTId6 = ROUT6.RID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_ShiftMst AS Scheduled ON Mx_DATDTrn.SchSFT = Scheduled.SFTID LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_ShiftMst AS Working ON Mx_DATDTrn.WrkSFT = Working.SFTID

  ===== dbo.Mx_VEW_RBMonthlyATDSummary (VIEW) =====
CREATE VIEW Mx_VEW_RBMonthlyATDSummary AS  SELECT AadharNo AS 'Aadhar No.' 	,Mx_ATDSUMTrn.ABDays AS 'Absent Days' 	,case when Mx_ATDSUMTrn.DaysOfMonth > 0 then ROUND(((Mx_ATDSUMTrn.ABDays * 100)/Mx_ATDSUMTrn.DaysOfMonth),2,1) else 0 end AS 'Absent Days(%)' 	,Mx_ATDSALSumTrn.PREVDays AS 'Adjusted Days' 	,Mx_ATDSALSumTrn.PREVOT AS 'Adjusted OT-Mins' 	,CASE WHEN LEN(CAST(CAST(Mx_ATDSALSumTrn.PREVOT / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6)) ) = 1            THEN RIGHT(REPLICATE('0', 2) +  CAST(CAST(Mx_ATDSALSumTrn.PREVOT / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6)),2)            ELSE CAST(CAST(Mx_ATDSALSumTrn.PREVOT / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6))  END 	 + ':' + RIGHT(REPLICATE('0', 2) + CAST(Mx_ATDSALSumTrn.PREVOT - CAST(Mx_ATDSALSumTrn.PREVOT / 60 AS INT) * 60 AS VARCHAR(6)), 2) AS 'Adjusted OT-HHMM' 	,Mx_ATDSALSumTrn.PREVSFTALW AS 'Adjusted Shift ALW' 	,Mx_ATDSALSumTrn.PREVWRKTime AS 'Adjusted Work Dur-Mins' 	,CASE WHEN LEN(CAST(CAST(Mx_ATDSALSumTrn.PREVWRKTime / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6)) ) = 1            THEN RIGHT(REPLICATE('0', 2) +  CAST(CAST(Mx_ATDSALSumTrn.PREVWRKTime / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6)),2)            ELSE CAST(CAST(Mx_ATDSALSumTrn.PREVWRKTime / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6))  END 	 + ':' + RIGHT(REPLICATE('0', 2) + CAST(Mx_ATDSALSumTrn.PREVWRKTime - CAST(Mx_ATDSALSumTrn.PREVWRKTime / 60 AS INT) * 60 AS VARCHAR(6)), 2) AS 'Adjusted Work Dur-HHMM' 	,Mx_UserMst.ATDPLCID AS 'Attendance Policy ID' 	,CASE WHEN LEN(CAST(CAST(Mx_ATDSUMTrn.AUTHCOFF / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6)) ) = 1            THEN RIGHT(REPLICATE('0', 2) +  CAST(CAST(Mx_ATDSUMTrn.AUTHCOFF / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6)),2)            ELSE CAST(CAST(Mx_ATDSUMTrn.AUTHCOFF / 60 AS INT) AS VARCHAR(6))  END 	 + ':' + RIGHT(REPLICATE('0', 2) +  CAST(Mx_ATDSUMTrn.AUTHCOFF - JOIN Mx_AttendancePolicyMst ON Mx_UserMst.ATDPLCID = Mx_AttendancePolicyMst.ATDPLCID   INNER JOIN Mx_BranchMst ON Mx_UserMst.BRCID = Mx_BranchMst.BRCID   INNER JOIN Mx_CategoryMst ON Mx_UserMst.CTGID = Mx_CategoryMst.CTGID   INNER JOIN Mx_CustomGroup1Mst ON Mx_UserMst.CG1ID = Mx_CustomGroup1Mst.CG1ID   INNER JOIN Mx_CustomGroup2Mst ON Mx_UserMst.CG2ID = Mx_CustomGroup2Mst.CG2ID  INNER JOIN Mx_CustomGroup3Mst ON Mx_UserMst.CG3ID = Mx_CustomGroup3Mst.CG3ID   INNER JOIN Mx_DepartmentMst ON Mx_UserMst.DPTID = Mx_DepartmentMst.DPTID   INNER JOIN Mx_DesignationMst ON Mx_UserMst.DSGID = Mx_DesignationMst.DSGID   INNER JOIN Mx_GradeMst ON Mx_UserMst.GRDID = Mx_GradeMst.GRDID   INNER JOIN Mx_SectionMst ON Mx_UserMst.SECID = Mx_SectionMst.SECID   INNER JOIN Mx_OrganizationMst ON Mx_UserMst.ORGID = Mx_OrganizationMst.ORGID   LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_ErrorMst ON Mx_DATDTrn.ErrorID = Mx_ErrorMst.ErrID   LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_VEW_OvertimeDet ON Mx_DATDTrn.UserID = Mx_VEW_OvertimeDet.userid AND Mx_DATDTrn.PDate = Mx_VEW_OvertimeDet.pdate  LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_RepoGroupMst ON Mx_UserMst.RGID = Mx_RepoGroupMst.RGID  LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS RIN1 ON Mx_DATDTrn.RINId1 = RIN1.RID  LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS RIN2 ON Mx_DATDTrn.RINId2 = RIN2.RID  LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS RIN3 ON Mx_DATDTrn.RINId3 = RIN3.RID  LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS RIN4 ON Mx_DATDTrn.RINId4 = RIN4.RID  LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS RIN5 ON Mx_DATDTrn.RINId5 = RIN5.RID  LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS RIN6 ON Mx_DATDTrn.RINId6 = RIN6.RID  LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS ROUT1 ON Mx_DATDTrn.ROUTId1 = ROUT1.RID  LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS ROUT2 ON Mx_DATDTrn.ROUTId2 = ROUT2.RID  LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS ROUT3 ON Mx_DATDTrn.ROUTId3 = ROUT3.RID  LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS ROUT4 ON Mx_DATDTrn.ROUTId4 = ROUT4.RID  LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS ROUT5 ON Mx_DATDTrn.ROUTId5 = ROUT5.RID  LEFT OUTER JOIN Mx_MovementReasonMst AS ROUT6 ON Mx_DATDTrn.ROUTId6 = ROUT6.RID   where Mx_DATDTrn.PDate >= Mx_ATDSUMTrn.FromDate  and  Mx_DATDTrn.PDate <=  Mx_ATDSUMTrn.ToDate

  ===== dbo.Mx_VEW_UserDetails (VIEW) =====
CREATE VIEW Mx_VEW_UserDetails  AS   SELECT Mx_UserMst.UserID as UserID, Mx_UserMst.Name as UserName, Mx_UserMst.IntegrationRef as Integration_Ref, Mx_UserMst.AdlUserID as ReferenceID, Mx_UserMst.DPTID as DptID, Mx_DepartmentMst.DptCode as DptCode, Mx_DepartmentMst.Name as DeptName, Mx_UserMst.SECID as SecID, Mx_SectionMst.SecCode as SecCode, Mx_SectionMst.Name as SecName, Mx_UserMst.CTGID as CtgID, Mx_CategoryMst.CtgCode as CtgCode, Mx_CategoryMst.Name as CtgName, Mx_UserMst.GRDID as GrdID, Mx_GradeMst.GrdCode as GrdCode, Mx_GradeMst.Name as GrdName, Mx_UserMst.DSGID as DsgID, Mx_DesignationMst.DsgCode as DsgCode, Mx_DesignationMst.Name as DsgName, Mx_CustomGroup1Mst.CG1ID CustomGroup1ID, Mx_CustomGroup1Mst.CG1CODE CustomGroup1_Code, Mx_CustomGroup1Mst.Name AS CustomGroup1_Name, Mx_CustomGroup2Mst.CG2ID CustomGroup2ID, Mx_CustomGroup2Mst.CG2CODE CustomGroup2_Code, Mx_CustomGroup2Mst.Name AS CustomGroup2_Name, Mx_CustomGroup3Mst.CG3ID CustomGroup3ID, Mx_CustomGroup3Mst.CG3CODE CustomGroup3_Code, Mx_CustomGroup3Mst.Name AS CustomGroup3_Name, Mx_UserMst.Card1 as Card1, Mx_UserMst.Card2 as Card2, Mx_UserMst.UserPIN as UserPin, Mx_UserMst.UserType as UserType, Mx_UserMst.UserIDEnbl as ActiveFlag, Mx_UserMst.Gender as Gender, Mx_UserMst.BldGrp as BldGrp, Mx_UserMst.MRTLSTAT as MaritalStatus, Mx_UserMst.Height as Height, Mx_UserMst.Weight as Weight, Mx_UserMst.Qualification as Qualification, Mx_UserMst.Experience as Experience, Mx_UserMst.LCLADD1 as LocalAdd1, Mx_UserMst.LCLADD2 as LocalAdd2, Mx_UserMst.LCLADD3 as LocalAddStreet, Mx_UserMst.LCLCity as LocalAddCity, Mx_UserMst.LCLPIN as LocalAddPIN, Mx_UserMst.LCLSTATE as LocalAddState, Mx_UserMst.LCLCOUNTRY as LocalAddCountry, Mx_UserMst.PERMADD1 as PermAdd1, VoterID, CONVERT(nvarchar(10),Mx_UserMst.UserValidityDate,103) as AccessValidity, Mx_UserMst.BlacklistFlag as BlacklistFlag, Mx_UserMst.LastModified as LastModified, Mx_UserMst.FullName from Mx_UserMst INNER JOIN Mx_OrganizationMst ON Mx_UserMst.ORGID = Mx_OrganizationMst.ORGID INNER JOIN Mx_BranchMst ON Mx_UserMst.BRCID = Mx_BranchMst.BRCID INNER JOIN Mx_DesignationMst ON Mx_UserMst.DsgID = Mx_DesignationMst.DsgID INNER JOIN Mx_DepartmentMst ON Mx_UserMst.DPTID = Mx_DepartmentMst.DPTID INNER JOIN Mx_SectionMst ON Mx_UserMst.SECID = Mx_SectionMst.SECID INNER JOIN Mx_CategoryMst ON Mx_UserMst.CTGID = Mx_CategoryMst.CTGID INNER JOIN Mx_GradeMst ON Mx_UserMst.GRDID = Mx_GradeMst.GRDID INNER JOIN Mx_CustomGroup1Mst ON Mx_UserMst.CG1ID = Mx_CustomGroup1Mst.CG1ID INNER JOIN Mx_CustomGroup2Mst ON Mx_UserMst.CG2ID = Mx_CustomGroup2Mst.CG2ID INNER JOIN Mx_CustomGroup3Mst ON Mx_UserMst.CG3ID = Mx_CustomGroup3Mst.CG3ID where Mx_UserMst.UserType='U'
[6] ANALYSIS -- where the License Information screen values come from

  Frontend flow (from the captured JS):
    licenseInformationController -> o.pageLoad()
    licenseInformationService.pageLoad -> u.executeApi("get", i.pageLoad, {})
    i = licenseInformationAPI  (the API-path registry object)
    Controller consumes: n.result.AppMode, n.result.MaxLicenseUsers,
                         n.result.LabelActiveUsers, n.gridData
    openFile() -> returns FilePath, opens EULA.pdf

  Database evidence:
    - Only ONE genuine licensing table exists: dbo.Mx_LicenseUpdateTrn
        columns: Id, UpdateDate, LoginUser, OldLicenseKey, NewLicenseKey
        rows: a single audit row (Id=1) recording the key that was set in 2017.
        => This is an AUDIT/HISTORY of license-key *changes*, not live capacity.
    - NO table/column named MaxLicenseUsers, AppMode, AllowedLimit, ActiveUsers,
      LabelActiveUsers exists anywhere in the database. (have_capacity_cols=False)
    - NO stored procedure / function / view / trigger references
      Mx_LicenseUpdateTrn, MaxLicenseUsers, AppMode, AllowedLimit or ActiveUsers.
    - The 6 programmable objects matched in section [1] are attendance REPORT
      VIEWS that merely contain columns like [Driving License] / [VISA Expiry
      Date] -- false positives on the words 'License'/'Expiry', NOT license logic.

  Conclusion (educational):
    AppMode and MaxLicenseUsers are NOT read from the database. The persisted
    NewLicenseKey string is an encoded/signed license blob; the COSEC server-side
    application decodes it in memory to derive the app mode and the licensed user
    ceiling. LabelActiveUsers is almost certainly a live COUNT of enabled users
    (e.g. Mx_UserMst WHERE UserIDEnbl=1 / dltdflg=0), computed by the app and
    compared against the decoded ceiling. gridData is the per-module/feature
    entitlement list, also derived from the decoded key, not from a DB table.
    The database's only role in licensing is the Mx_LicenseUpdateTrn audit trail.

  API endpoint (Task 3) -- NOT resolvable from this repo:
    The value of licenseInformationAPI.pageLoad and .openFile is assigned inside
    the COSEC frontend JS bundle, which is NOT present in this project. To recover
    the exact URLs, search the captured bundle(s) for the token
    'licenseInformationAPI' and its 'pageLoad:' / 'openFile:' property
    assignments (often a separate *api*.js / endpoints bundle). Typical COSEC
    web paths are under http://192.168.10.14/COSEC/... but the literal path
    cannot be asserted without the bundle.

  Server-side implementation (Task 4) -- NOT available:
    COSEC is a third-party Matrix product; its server source (controller/action
    -> service -> data access) is not part of this repository, so the route ->
    controller -> SP chain cannot be traced here. Nothing in this repo implements
    the License Information endpoint.

[7] FIELD FLOW MAPPING (browser -> API -> server -> SQL -> DB)

    AppMode          -> n.result.AppMode        -> COSEC app (decode NewLicenseKey) -> NO DB column
    MaxLicenseUsers  -> n.result.MaxLicenseUsers -> COSEC app (decode NewLicenseKey) -> NO DB column
    LabelActiveUsers -> n.result.LabelActiveUsers-> COSEC app (live count)           -> likely COUNT(Mx_UserMst)
    gridData         -> n.gridData               -> COSEC app (decode key/entitlements) -> NO DB table
    NewLicenseKey    -> (audit only)             -> stored verbatim                 -> dbo.Mx_LicenseUpdateTrn.NewLicenseKey
    EULA FilePath    -> openFile() result        -> COSEC app (filesystem path)     -> NOT in DB (serves EULA.pdf)

[8] GAPS / NOT FOUND

    - Exact licenseInformationAPI.pageLoad / .openFile URLs: JS bundle not in repo.
    - Server-side controller/service/repository: COSEC (Matrix) product source not in repo.
    - MaxLicenseUsers / AppMode / gridData source rows: not in DB (decoded from key at runtime).
